D2R=Math.PI/180,R2D=180/Math.PI;\nconst sinD=a=>Math.sin(a*D2R),cosD=a=>Math.cos(a*D2R),tanD=a=>Math.tan(a*D2R);\nconst asinD=x=>Math.asin(Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,x)))*R2D,acosD=x=>Math.acos(Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,x)))*R2D;\nconst atan2D=(y,x)=>Math.atan2(y,x)*R2D;\nconst wrap=(a,b)=>{a=a-b*Math.floor(a/b);return a<0?a+b:a;};\nconst fixAng=a=>wrap(a,360),fixHr=a=>wrap(a,24);\nconst clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));\n\nfunction julianDay(y,m,d){if(m<=2){y-=1;m+=12;}const A=Math.floor(y/100),B=2-A+Math.floor(A/4);\n  return Math.floor(365.25*(y+4716))+Math.floor(30.6001*(m+1))+d+B-1524.5;}\nfunction sunPos(jd){const D=jd-2451545;\n  const g=fixAng(357.529+0.98560028*D),q=fixAng(280.459+0.98564736*D);\n  const L=fixAng(q+1.915*sinD(g)+0.020*sinD(2*g));\n  const e=23.439-0.00000036*D;\n  const ra=fixHr(atan2D(cosD(e)*sinD(L),cosD(L))/15);\n  return {decl:asinD(sinD(e)*sinD(L)),eqt:q/15-ra,ra:ra,obl:e,lam:L};}\n\nconst METHODS={\n  mwl:{fajr:18,isha:17,asr:1,label:'Muslim World League · 18° al-Qura · Makkah'},\n  isna:{fajr:15,isha:15,asr:1,label:'ISNA · 15° / 15°'},\n  hanafi:{fajr:18,isha:17,asr:2,label:'Muslim World League · Ḥanafī ʿAṣr'},\n  kurdistan:{fajr:16.5,isha:0,ishaMin:79,asr:1,label:'Kurdistan · local mosque table',\n    off:{fajr:1,sunrise:9,dhuhr:11,asr:7,maghrib:3,isha:0}}\n};\nfunction dayTimes(date,lat,lng,tz,p){\n  const jd=julianDay(date.getFullYear(),date.getMonth()+1,date.getDate())-lng/360;\n  const midDay=t=>fixHr(12-sunPos(jd+t).eqt);\n  const angTime=(G,t,ccw)=>{const d=sunPos(jd+t).decl;\n    const c=(-sinD(G)-sinD(d)*sinD(lat))/(cosD(d)*cosD(lat));\n    if(c>1||c<-1)return NaN;\n    const v=acosD(c)/15;return midDay(t)+(ccw?-v:v);};\n  const asrTime=t=>{const d=sunPos(jd+t).decl;\n    return angTime(-(Math.atan(1/(p.asr+tanD(Math.abs(lat-d))))*R2D),t,false);};\n  const sf=(v,fb)=>isFinite(v)?v/24:fb;\n  let t={fajr:5/24,sunrise:6/24,dhuhr:12/24,asr:13/24,sunset:18/24,isha:19/24};\n  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){\n    const n={fajr:sf(angTime(p.fajr,t.fajr,true),t.fajr),sunrise:sf(angTime(0.833,t.sunrise,true),t.sunrise),\n      dhuhr:sf(midDay(t.dhuhr),t.dhuhr),asr:sf(asrTime(t.asr),t.asr),\n      sunset:sf(angTime(0.833,t.sunset,false),t.sunset),isha:t.isha};\n    n.isha=p.ishaMin?n.sunset+p.ishaMin/1440:sf(angTime(p.isha,t.isha,false),t.isha);\n    t=n;}\n  const off=tz-lng/15;\n  const o={fajr:t.fajr*24+off,sunrise:t.sunrise*24+off,dhuhr:t.dhuhr*24+off+1/60,\n    asr:t.asr*24+off,maghrib:t.sunset*24+off+1/60,isha:t.isha*24+off,jd:jd,adj:false};\n  // High-latitude safety: angle-based portions of the night when the sun never\n  // reaches the twilight angle (Scandinavia in summer, etc.)\n  if(isFinite(o.sunrise)&&isFinite(o.maghrib)){\n    const night=24-(o.maghrib-o.sunrise);\n    const fLim=night*(p.fajr/60),iLim=night*((p.ishaMin?18:p.isha)/60);\n    if(!isFinite(o.fajr)||(o.sunrise-o.fajr)>fLim){o.fajr=o.sunrise-fLim;o.adj=true;}\n    if(!isFinite(o.isha)||(o.isha-o.maghrib)>iLim){o.isha=o.maghrib+iLim;o.adj=true;}\n  }\n  return o;\n}\n\nconst
